        <div data-pjax="true" data-test-selector="body-content" data-view-component="true" class="markdown-body tmp-my-3"><h1>v0.34.0</h1>
<p><strong>1,061 commits from 412 contributors (120 new).</strong> Thank you!</p>
<h2>TL;DR</h2>
<ul>
<li><strong>10 new models</strong> (Mistral Large 4, MiniMax-M3.1-Flash, Nemotron-3.5-Transcribe, and more!)</li>
<li><strong>Extensible KV cache is on by default:</strong> vLLM sizes the KV cache from memory actually free after warmup, so most models get a bigger cache and more throughput - in most cases you should now <strong>remove <code>gpu_memory_utilization</code></strong> from your configs.</li>
<li><strong>Model Runner V1 is gone.</strong> Model Runner V2 is now the only GPU runner.</li>
<li><strong>Big speedups</strong> on Blackwell and MI355X: up to 1.95× decode (Step5 on GB200), 1.6× throughput (GLM-5.3-Flash), 1.24–1.40× faster long-prompt TTFT (GLM-5.3).</li>
<li><strong>Upgrade check:</strong> default block size is now 64, ROCm 7.2 images are gone, and <code>huggingface_hub &gt;= 2.0.0</code> is required. See <a href="#before-you-upgrade">Before you upgrade</a>.</li>
</ul>
<h2>Before you upgrade</h2>
<div class="markdown-alert markdown-alert-important"><p class="markdown-alert-title"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-report mr-2" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M0 1.75C0 .784.784 0 1.75 0h12.5C15.216 0 16 .784 16 1.75v9.5A1.75 1.75 0 0 1 14.25 13H8.06l-2.573 2.573A1.458 1.458 0 0 1 3 14.543V13H1.75A1.75 1.75 0 0 1 0 11.25Zm1.75-.25a.25.25 0 0 0-.25.25v9.5c0 .138.112.25.25.25h2a.75.75 0 0 1 .75.75v2.19l2.72-2.72a.749.749 0 0 1 .53-.22h6.5a.25.25 0 0 0 .25-.25v-9.5a.25.25 0 0 0-.25-.25Zm7 2.25v2.5a.75.75 0 0 1-1.5 0v-2.5a.75.75 0 0 1 1.5 0ZM9 9a1 1 0 1 1-2 0 1 1 0 0 1 2 0Z"></path></svg>Important</p><p>These changes can break existing setups.</p>
</div>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Change</th>
<th>What to do</th>
<th>PR</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>Model Runner V1 removed</td>
<td>Remove <code>VLLM_USE_V2_MODEL_RUNNER=0</code>; it now fails at startup. Port V1 logits processors to <code>vllm.v1.worker.gpu.sample.logits_processor</code>.</td>
<td><a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6001962406" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/63373" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63373/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63373">#63373</a></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Default <code>--block-size</code> is 64 (was 16)</td>
<td>Pass <code>--block-size 16</code> to keep the old behavior. ROCm paged attention still uses 16.</td>
<td><a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5989713643" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/63231" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63231/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63231">#63231</a></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Extensible KV cache on by default; <code>gpu_memory_utilization</code> defaults to 1.0</td>
<td><strong>In most cases it's now best to not set <code>gpu_memory_utilization</code></strong>. This should provide maximum kv cache capacity while avoiding runtime OOMs. Use it to share a GPU with other processes or when extensible kv cache is disabled.</td>
<td><a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5427044802" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/56492" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56492/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56492">#56492</a></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>ROCm 7.2 artifacts dropped</td>
<td>Use the ROCm 10.0 wheels and images. <code>-rocm72</code> is no longer published.</td>
<td><a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5877058226" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/61939" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61939/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61939">#61939</a></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td><code>huggingface_hub &gt;= 2.0.0</code> required</td>
<td>Also requires <code>tokenizers &gt;= 0.23.3</code> and <code>timm &gt;= 1.0.30</code>.</td>
<td><a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6123285409" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/64779" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64779/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64779">#64779</a></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>QuTLASS / <code>fp_quant</code> removed</td>
<td><code>fp_quant</code> checkpoints no longer load.</td>
<td><a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6170612057" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/65230" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65230/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65230">#65230</a></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Marlin W4A8 removed</td>
<td>Use <code>--quantization humming</code> with <code>VLLM_HUMMING_INPUT_QUANT_CONFIG</code> instead of <code>VLLM_MARLIN_INPUT_DTYPE</code>.</td>
<td><a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6194757596" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/65542" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65542/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65542">#65542</a></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>Keye-VL and Keye-VL-1.5 removed</td>
<td>—</td>
<td><a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6080460497" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/64287" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64287/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64287">#64287</a></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>
<p>All breaking changes, deprecations and new defaults are listed in <a href="#full-changelog">the full changelog</a>.</p>

<h2>Highlights</h2>
<h3>Extensible KV cache, on by default</h3>
<ul>
<li>On CUDA and ROCm, the KV cache is sized from memory actually free <strong>after</strong> CUDA graph capture and warmup, not from an estimate (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5427044802" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/56492" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56492/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56492">#56492</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Result:</strong> a larger cache (e.g. MiniMax-M3 on H200: 433k → 672k tokens), so throughput is higher and TTFT lower at high concurrency.</li>
<li><strong>Trade-off:</strong> per-token decode latency can rise on some models (TPOT +6–18% on some H200 models at concurrency 128). Opt out with <code>--gpu-memory-utilization 0.9</code> or <code>--no-enable-extensible-kv-cache</code>.</li>
<li>Hardened with fixes for allocator fragmentation, startup OOMs and KV connectors (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6357025853" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/67296" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/67296/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/67296">#67296</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6371726616" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/67485" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/67485/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/67485">#67485</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6301369634" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/66731" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66731/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66731">#66731</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6283370729" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/66523" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66523/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66523">#66523</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6252993110" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/66197" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66197/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66197">#66197</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6160438255" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/65137" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65137/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65137">#65137</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6290027007" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/66605" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66605/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66605">#66605</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6297028926" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/66674" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66674/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66674">#66674</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Model Runner V2 only</h3>
<ul>
<li>Model Runner V1 is removed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6001962406" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/63373" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63373/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63373">#63373</a>).</li>
<li>Dual-batch overlap now runs on MRV2, including with speculative decoding and DCP (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5834624863" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/61464" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61464/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61464">#61464</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h3>Speculative decoding</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Suffix decoding</strong> runs on MRV2 with a GPU speculator; Arctic Inference is no longer needed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5739068331" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/60339" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60339/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60339">#60339</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Custom speculators:</strong> declarative configs plus a <code>register_speculative_method</code> plugin API (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5987497143" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/63205" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63205/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63205">#63205</a>).</li>
<li><strong>CUDA graphs</strong> for MRV2 draft-model speculators (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5790137018" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/60989" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60989/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60989">#60989</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Adaptive verification</strong> works with XPress (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6164702770" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/65175" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65175/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65175">#65175</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Draft KV cache matches the target's dtype</strong>, so an FP8 target gets an FP8 draft cache (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6167038206" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/65198" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65198/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65198">#65198</a>).</li>
</ul>

</ul>
<h3>Serving and operations</h3>
<ul>
<li><strong>Checkpoint/restore</strong> (CRIU + <code>cuda-checkpoint</code>) through <code>/checkpoint/prepare</code>, <code>/checkpoint/status</code> and <code>/checkpoint/restore</code>, in both the Python and Rust frontends (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5932867296" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/62570" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62570/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/62570">#62570</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Fault-tolerant DP+EP scale-down:</strong> dead ranks are removed and their experts redistributed without a restart (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4716802452" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/46370" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46370/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/46370">#46370</a>).</li>
<li>Best-effort <strong>NUMA binding</strong> on by default (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5817969699" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/61258" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61258/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61258">#61258</a>).</li>
<li>Opt-in <strong>cache-aware admission</strong> ordering (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5304394203" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/54625" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54625/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/54625">#54625</a>).</li>
<li>Rust frontend: stateless <strong>Responses API</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6051480795" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/63902" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63902/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63902">#63902</a>) and <strong><code>n &gt; 1</code></strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6199247869" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/65625" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65625/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65625">#65625</a>).</li>
</ul>
<h2>Install</h2>
<p><strong>Python wheels</strong></p>
<table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th>Platform</th>
<th>Install</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td>CUDA 13.0 (PyPI)</td>
<td><code>pip install vllm</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>CUDA 13.0 (uv)</td>
<td><code>uv pip install vllm --torch-backend=auto</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>ROCm 10.0</td>
<td><code>pip install vllm --extra-index-url https://wheels.vllm.ai/rocm/0.34.0/rocm100</code></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>XPU</td>
<td><code>uv pip install vllm --extra-index-url https://wheels.vllm.ai/0.34.0/xpu --extra-index-url https://download.pytorch.org/whl/xpu --index-strategy unsafe-best-match</code></td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

<p><code>latest</code> tags work the same way (e.g. <code>vllm/vllm:latest-rocm</code>). The default CUDA image now includes the AWS EFA userspace (libfabric, aws-ofi-nccl). The <code>-efa</code> image adds DeepEP built for EFA and routes NCCL over EFA by default (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6195998571" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/65576" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65576/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65576">#65576</a>).</p>
<p>The <strong>Assets</strong> section below has the source tarball and the CUDA 13.0 (x86_64, arm64), CPU (x86_64, arm64, macOS) and XPU (x86_64) wheels.</p>
<h2>Full changelog</h2>
<details>
<summary><b>Model support</b></summary>
<ul>

<li><strong>Extensible KV cache</strong>

<summary><b>All breaking changes, deprecations and new defaults</b></summary>
<ul>
<li><strong>Model Runner V1 removed</strong> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6001962406" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/63373" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63373/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63373">#63373</a>).
<ul>
<li><code>VLLM_USE_V2_MODEL_RUNNER</code> and <code>VllmConfig.use_v2_model_runner</code> are gone; setting the env var to anything but <code>1</code> fails at startup.</li>
<li>Dropped with it: the V1 logits-processor API (<code>vllm.v1.worker.gpu.sample.logits_processor</code> is the only interface), the <code>custom_class</code> speculative method, <code>use_heterogeneous_vocab</code>, <code>disable_padded_drafter_batch</code>, <code>parallel_drafting</code> outside DFlash/DSpark, PP with <code>external_launcher</code>, <code>NGramPerReqLogitsProcessor</code>, and x86 CPUs without Triton.</li>
<li>Deprecated: <code>parallel_drafting</code>, <code>capture_torch_profiler</code>, <code>enable_layerwise_nvtx_tracing</code>.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Default <code>--block-size</code> is 64</strong> instead of 16 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5989713643" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/63231" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63231/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63231">#63231</a>). Backends with a preferred block size still override it (ROCm paged attention keeps 16), and an explicit <code>--block-size</code> still wins.</li>
<li><strong><code>gpu_memory_utilization</code> defaults to 1.0</strong> when unset with the extensible KV cache on (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5427044802" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/56492" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56492/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56492">#56492</a>). An explicit value in (0.8, 1.0) that withholds 1 GiB or more logs a warning (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6299611127" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/66704" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66704/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/66704">#66704</a>).</li>
<li><strong>ROCm 7.2</strong> wheels and <code>-rocm72</code> images are no longer published (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5877058226" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/61939" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61939/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61939">#61939</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Removed:</strong>
<ul>
<li>QuTLASS and the <code>fp_quant</code> method (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6170612057" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/65230" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65230/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65230">#65230</a>).</li>
<li>Marlin INT8/FP8-activation (W4A8) paths and <code>VLLM_MARLIN_INPUT_DTYPE</code>; use <code>--quantization humming</code> with <code>VLLM_HUMMING_INPUT_QUANT_CONFIG</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6194757596" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/65542" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65542/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65542">#65542</a>).</li>
<li>Keye-VL and Keye-VL-1.5 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6080460497" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/64287" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64287/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64287">#64287</a>); <code>disable_mtp_completeness_check</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="4949882720" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/49459" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49459/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/49459">#49459</a>); the <code>refresh_derived_weights</code> hook (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6223613186" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/65914" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65914/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65914">#65914</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Dependencies:</strong> <code>huggingface_hub &gt;= 2.0.0</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6123285409" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/64779" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64779/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64779">#64779</a>); <code>mistral-common &gt;= 1.13.0</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6070273003" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/64169" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64169/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64169">#64169</a>); XPU requires vllm_xpu_kernels 0.1.17 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5760006496" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/60609" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60609/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/60609">#60609</a>).</li>
<li><strong>Model and plugin code:</strong> all <code>process_weights_after_loading</code> hooks share one signature and the loader runs every module's hook (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5508943894" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/57678" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57678/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57678">#57678</a>); config <code>compute_hash()</code> replaced by <code>compile_factors()</code> / <code>hash_config()</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6201213651" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/65652" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65652/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65652">#65652</a>).</li>
<li><strong>API behavior:</strong>
<ul>
<li>llama3_json no longer streams tool arguments incrementally (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6023411270" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/63569" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63569/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63569">#63569</a>).</li>
<li>Canary transcription without <code>language</code> returns 400 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6187256403" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/65445" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65445/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65445">#65445</a>).</li>
<li>An empty <code>tools</code> array is accepted in both frontends (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6194796198" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/65547" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65547/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65547">#65547</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6219150446" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/65854" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65854/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65854">#65854</a>).</li>
<li><code>/v1/systemone</code> rejects request-supplied chat templates without <code>--trust-request-chat-template</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6098038065" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/64503" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64503/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64503">#64503</a>).</li>
<li>LoRA adapters on modules vLLM does not wrap are refused (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6014680882" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/63489" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63489/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63489">#63489</a>); misconfigured NCCL weight transfer raises (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5463425883" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/57022" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57022/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/57022">#57022</a>).</li>
<li><code>vllm-rs serve</code> no longer reads <code>--data-parallel-rank</code> from <code>VLLM_DP_RANK</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6180889158" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/65369" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65369/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65369">#65369</a>).</li>
<li>AITER fused shared experts with MiniMax M3 and DeepSeek V4 need <code>VLLM_ROCM_USE_AITER=1</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5834228976" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/61457" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61457/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61457">#61457</a>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Still deprecated:</strong> <code>--mamba-cache-mode</code> has no effect; its removal was postponed (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6129540102" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/64838" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64838/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64838">#64838</a>).</li>
<li><strong>New defaults:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Extensible KV cache (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5427044802" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/56492" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56492/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/56492">#56492</a>); block size 64 (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5989713643" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/63231" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63231/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63231">#63231</a>); best-effort NUMA binding (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5817969699" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/61258" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61258/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/61258">#61258</a>).</li>
<li>Token-sharded sparse-MLA prefill at 1024 or more prefill tokens (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6053832832" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/63956" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63956/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63956">#63956</a>); MLA decode up-projection before the A2A merge (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6148110770" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/64994" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64994/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/64994">#64994</a>).</li>
<li><code>VLLM_COMPILE_ON_ONE_RANK</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="5990569718" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/63247" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63247/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/63247">#63247</a>); minimum valid EPLB <code>num_redundant_experts</code> (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="3726067510" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/30614" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30614/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/30614">#30614</a>).</li>
<li>HiSparse on the extensible KV cache (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6168899163" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/issues/65214" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65214/hovercard" href="https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm/pull/65214">#65214</a>); encoder CUDA graphs up to <code>encoder_cudagraph_max_token_budget=5120</code>, eager above (#65227).</li>
<li>TRITON_ATTN on RDNA3/RDNA4 (#60418); MRV2 on non-Triton CPUs (#61995); <code>float32</code> Mamba SSM cache for Nemotron Omni (#63218); Zipf routing in <code>vllm bench forward</code> (#64167).</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
